\x1a\x06:Djinni selection
Djinn

Psynergy
The four elements are\x03the basic building\x03blocks of all matter.\x01The elemental spirits\x03are called Djinn.\x01The four elements are\x03Venus for Earth,\x03Mercury for Water,\x01Mars for Fire, and\x03Jupiter for Wind.\x01Each element has many\x03Djinn.
When you set a Djinni, its\x03elemental power may\x03change your class\x01or Psynergy. In battle,\x03you can unleash the\x03powers of set Djinn.
When a Djinni is standing\x03by, you can use it\x03to summon in battle.\x01To use a summon\x03immediately, put your\x03Djinn on standby before\x01battle. But be careful,\x03because changing set\x03Djinn to standby changes\x01your class and Psynergy.
If you have a standby\x03Djinni in battle, you\x03can use it to summon.\x01After summoning, the\x03Djinni will be in\x03recovery until it has\x01recovered all its\x03power. Recovering Djinni\x03cannot be unleashed\x01or used to summon.
If a Djinni's power is\x03used to summon, the\x03Djinni enters recovery.\x01After it has recovered\x03its power, it will set\x03itself once again.
When a Djinni is set to\x03an Adept, its power\x03may change the Adept's\x01class. Find your own\x03unique combinations of\x03Djinni to improve your\x01characters' classes.
Psynergy is a mental\x03power. People who wield\x03it are called Adepts.\x01Psynergy can change\x03with class changes or\x03when you go up a level.\x01Try changing your\x03class to earn new\x03Psynergy.
To give your Djinni to\x03someone else, move it\x03like this.\x02
After choosing who will\x03take the Djinni, you\x03can set it.\x02
When your class changes,\x03the Psynergy you can use\x03may also change.\x02
Setting a Djinni changes\x03your attributes and\x03maybe even your class.\x02
Each Djinni has\x03different powers.\x1e
Once we are set, you\x03can unleash us to\x03use our powers.\x1e
So, let me demonstrate\x03the power of my attack,\x03\x11\x01.\x1e
See! When you unleashed\x03me, I attacked with my\x03mighty stone-cleaver!\x02
After being unleashed,\x03we Djinn will then\x03stand by to be summoned...\x1e
If you have standby\x03Djinn, you can summon\x03even greater powers!\x1e
Choose this command\x03to unleash me in\x03battle, OK?\x1e
